effect flying_ember		// for flying ember effect in maps in connection with "flying_dust"-feature
countabsolute 0.5		
type snow
tex 56 58
color 0x701010 0xDD6020  
size 0.4 0.7
alpha 75 150 80
originoffset 0 0 0           
originjitter 90 90 90
airfriction 5
velocityoffset 0 0 50 


effect flying_dust		// for flying dust effect in maps in connection with "flying_dust"-feature

tex 56 58
color 0x503025 0x503525  
size 0.2 0.7
alpha 75 150 100
originoffset 0 0 0           
originjitter 90 90 90
airfriction 5

originoffset 0 0 0
